# Agents · R · part 7

1,234 entries, README to README. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **README**: `plan`, `cook` and `fix` delegate to named roles: a researcher investigates, a planner writes the plan files, a reviewer red-teams it, a tester drives the suite green. Splitting the work this way is the point — a fresh context that only has to review is much harder to talk out of a finding than the same context that just wrote the code. ([nguyenquocan0101/skills](https://github.com/nguyenquocan0101/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/README.md) · ★1)

- **README**: 1. **Giới hạn tool là ràng buộc cứng.** `reviewer` không có `Edit`/`Write` thì **không thể** sửa code, dù bị dụ bằng bất cứ prompt nào.
